- **workflow-type-resolve**: Given a classified intent, decide whether to FOLLOW an existing reusable workflow type (a skill / command / playbook whose purpose fits this work) or CONSTRUCT the steps from scratch via the inner engine. Searches CHILD scope (company-local skills + commands + playbooks) before PLATFORM scope (the plugin skill catalog), and child-custody wins. Use right after the intent is classified (core:input-routing + core:human-sutra) and before you start building steps. Skip when you are already mid-step inside a resolved workflow, or for a pure read-only question with no work to shape. ([sankalpasawa/sutra](https://github.com/sankalpasawa/sutra/tree/HEAD/marketplace/plugin/skills/workflow-type-resolve) · ★1)
